```text
15	
16	Our analysis yields three findings. First, attention-driven convergence is task-specific. Of 10 benchmarks, only LAMBADA exhibits monotonic reduction in cross-architecture variance ($\rho = -1.0$, BCa 95\% CI $[-1.0, -0.7]$; cross-architecture $\sigma$ drops 79\%). LAMBADA \citep{paperno2016lambada} is a pure context-retrieval task that requires predicting the final word of a passage from long-range dependencies; attention provides direct token-level access to the full context, which offers a natural explanation for why convergence occurs on this task and not others. Benchmarks that involve reasoning or comprehension show no convergence, with all $|\rho| \leq 0.7$ and $p \geq .233$. The Bonferroni-adjusted $p = .100$ does not survive strict correction at $\alpha = 0.05$; the confidence interval, which excludes zero, provides a complementary characterization of the effect. Second, convergence depends on scale. At 1.3B parameters the $\sigma$ trajectory is monotonically decreasing, but at 340M it is non-monotonic: $\sigma$ partially rebounds at the highest attention ratio, with only 36\% net reduction versus 79\% at 1.3B. This contrast provides a preliminary observation that sufficient model capacity may facilitate complete equalization on retrieval. Third, and central to our title-level claim, aggregate convergence and per-example divergence are decoupled. Cross-architecture $\sigma$ on LAMBADA decreases gradually across attention ratios ($0.024 \to 0.016 \to 0.014 \to 0.006 \to 0.005$), but pairwise Jaccard similarity of per-example helped and hurt sets saturates at the first attention level (4\%) and remains flat at approximately 0.095. Within each architecture, pure-model confidence on the target token predicts which examples are helped or hurt ($d = 0.73$--$0.92$), but this predictability does not transfer across architectures. These two quantities operate on different timescales: aggregate performance equalizes progressively, while per-example specificity is established from the earliest attention allocation and does not change with more attention.
17	
18	Our contributions are as follows:
19	\begin{itemize}
20	    \item We quantify task-specific convergence: only retrieval tasks (LAMBADA) show attention-driven cross-architecture convergence at 1.3B scale; reasoning and comprehension benchmarks do not (\S\ref{ssec:task_convergence}, Table~\ref{tab:convergence}, Figure~\ref{fig:task_specificity}).
21	    \item We characterize scale-dependent convergence: the monotonic convergence pattern at 1.3B becomes non-monotonic at 340M, a preliminary observation that sufficient scale may facilitate complete equalization (\S\ref{ssec:scale_convergence}).
22	    \item We demonstrate that aggregate convergence and per-example divergence are decoupled: attention reduces cross-architecture variance gradually, but per-example overlap saturates at the first attention level and does not increase further, constituting a form of predictive multiplicity arising from architectural variation (\S\ref{ssec:decoupling}, Table~\ref{tab:per_example}, Figure~\ref{fig:decoupling}).
23	\end{itemize}
24	
```

<details><summary>↩ Tool result · `toolu_017HPvdJt63bhXGHEPivEtqe`</summary>

```text
50	
51	\subsection{Per-Example Divergence}\label{ssec:per_example}
52	
53	The aggregate convergence on LAMBADA raises a question: do architectures converge by improving on the same examples, or do they help and hurt different examples when attention is added? We compare each architecture's pure-recurrent baseline against its h3-1 hybrid variant on 5{,}153 LAMBADA examples, classifying each example as helped (correct only in hybrid), hurt (correct only in pure), or unchanged.
54	
55	Each architecture displays a distinct profile of per-example changes (Table~\ref{tab:per_example}). GDN loses a net 153 examples (462 helped, 615 hurt), RetNet loses 69 (426/495), and HGRN2 gains 153 (548/395). HGRN2 is the only architecture for which attention produces a net per-example benefit. Pairwise Jaccard similarity is low: mean $\bar{J} \leq 0.104$ for helped, $\leq 0.096$ for hurt, indicating ${\sim}90$\% non-overlap. Overlap significantly exceeds the independence baseline ($J_{\text{null}} \approx 0.05$, all $z > 5.9$, $p < .0001$; Appendix~\ref{app:jaccard_permutation}), but normalizing by the theoretical upper bound $J_{\max} = \min(|A|,|B|)/\max(|A|,|B|)$, observed overlap reaches only 5--7\% of the achievable range above chance (normalized ES 0.065 helped, 0.047 hurt; per-pair details in Appendix~\ref{app:normalized_es}), confirming that low Jaccard cannot be attributed to set-size constraints.
56	
57	No surface-level feature (token count, word frequency, syntactic complexity) reliably separates helped from hurt examples. However, pure-model confidence on the target token is strongly predictive within each architecture: hurt examples have lower target-token probability among pure-correct examples ($d = 0.83$--$0.92$), and helped examples have higher probability among pure-wrong examples ($d = 0.73$--$0.77$); see Appendix~\ref{app:confidence_margin}. A Kruskal-Wallis test confirms cross-architecture structure in both hurt ($p = .0005$) and helped ($p = .020$) distributions. This structured per-example disagreement despite similar aggregate performance constitutes predictive multiplicity \citep{marx2020predictive} arising from architectural variation rather than random initialization \citep{damour2022underspecification}.
58	
59	
60	\subsection{Decoupling}\label{ssec:decoupling}
61	
62	The central finding of this paper is that aggregate convergence and per-example divergence operate on different timescales as attention ratio increases (Figure~\ref{fig:decoupling}).
63	
64	Cross-architecture $\sigma$ decreases gradually across the five attention ratios: $0.024 \to 0.016 \to 0.014 \to 0.006 \to 0.005$. Each additional attention layer provides incremental reduction. In contrast, pairwise Jaccard similarity between architectures' per-example effects saturates at the first attention level (4\% attention): approximately 0.095 for helped examples, with no further increase at higher ratios. Net asymmetry between architectures' per-example profiles increases from 0.013 at 4\% attention to 0.040 at 25\%.
65	
66	This separation of timescales constitutes a decoupling: attention reduces the aggregate performance gap but does not homogenize per-example behavior. Bootstrap confidence intervals (10{,}000 resamples) confirm this statistically: the 95\% CI for $\sigma$ does not overlap between pure $[0.019, 0.030]$ and h3-1 $[0.002, 0.011]$, while the CIs for mean Jaccard overlap completely across all non-zero attention ratios (helped: $[0.08, 0.11]$; hurt: $[0.07, 0.11]$). Within-architecture Jaccard averages ${\sim}0.33$ (Appendix~\ref{app:within_arch_stability}), roughly three times the cross-architecture value, confirming that architecture identity has greater influence than attention dose on which examples are affected.
67	
68	Attention weight analysis (Appendix~\ref{app:attention_patterns}) provides partial insight: at layer 2, GDN and RetNet produce nearly identical attention distributions (cosine 0.995) yet differ in per-example effects; entropy differences between helped and hurt examples are negligible for GDN and HGRN2 ($|\Delta H| \leq 0.002$), with RetNet showing a small but slightly larger difference ($\Delta H = +0.018$). Per-example effects originate primarily in the recurrent layers.
69	
```

```text
68	Attention weight analysis (Appendix~\ref{app:attention_patterns}) provides partial insight: at layer 2, GDN and RetNet produce nearly identical attention distributions (cosine 0.995) yet differ in per-example effects; entropy differences between helped and hurt examples are negligible for GDN and HGRN2 ($|\Delta H| \leq 0.002$), with RetNet showing a small but slightly larger difference ($\Delta H = +0.018$). Per-example effects originate primarily in the recurrent layers.
69	
70	\subsection{Robustness}\label{ssec:robustness}
71	
72	Removing GDN and recomputing all correlations weakens the LAMBADA result from $\rho = -1.0$ to $\rho = -0.7$ ($p = .233$) and reverses MMLU from $\rho = -0.7$ to $\rho = +0.6$, confirming that individual architectures influence the aggregate trends. The weakening from $\rho = -1.0$ to $\rho = -0.7$ is confounded with the reduction from $n = 3$ to $n = 2$ architectures; with only two architectures, the minimum achievable $|\rho|$ for a non-perfect monotonic relationship is $0.5$, limiting statistical resolution.
73	
74	To test whether the decoupling survives without GDN, we examine the RetNet--HGRN2 pair: all hybrid $\sigma \leq 0.008$ ($>$54\% below pure, $\rho = -0.7$), while pairwise Jaccard saturates at 0.086--0.108 (helped) and 0.085--0.095 (hurt) with no increase across ratios. Both aggregate $\sigma$ reduction and Jaccard saturation hold, confirming the decoupling is not an artifact of the GDN head-count confound (Appendix~\ref{app:gdn_confound}).
75	
76	Full sensitivity analyses, including bootstrap degenerate-resample treatment, appear in Appendix~\ref{app:sensitivity}.
77
```

```json
          " ",
          " The aggregate convergence on LAMBADA raises a question: do architectures converge by improving on the same examples, or do they help and hurt different examples when attention is added? We compare each architecture's pure-recurrent baseline against its h3-1 hybrid variant on 5{,}153 LAMBADA examples, classifying each example as helped (correct only in hybrid), hurt (correct only in pure), or unchanged.",
          " ",
          "-Each architecture displays a distinct profile of per-example changes (Table~\\ref{tab:per_example}). GDN loses a net 153 examples (462 helped, 615 hurt), RetNet loses 69 (426/495), and HGRN2 gains 153 (548/395). HGRN2 is the only architecture for which attention produces a net per-example benefit. Pairwise Jaccard similarity is low: mean $\\bar{J} \\leq 0.104$ for helped, $\\leq 0.096$ for hurt, indicating ${\\sim}90$\\% non-overlap. Overlap significantly exceeds the independence baseline ($J_{\\text{null}} \\approx 0.05$, all $z > 5.9$, $p < .0001$; Appendix~\\ref{app:jaccard_permutation}), but normalizing by the theoretical upper bound $J_{\\max} = \\min(|A|,|B|)/\\max(|A|,|B|)$, observed overlap reaches only 5--7\\% of the achievable range above chance (normalized ES 0.065 helped, 0.047 hurt; per-pair details in Appendix~\\ref{app:normalized_es}), confirming that low Jaccard cannot be attributed to set-size constraints.",
          "+Each architecture displays a distinct profile of per-example changes (Table~\\ref{tab:per_example}). GDN loses a net 153 examples (462 helped, 615 hurt), RetNet loses 69 (426/495), and HGRN2 gains 153 (548/395). HGRN2 is the only architecture for which attention produces a net per-example benefit. Pairwise Jaccard similarity is low: mean $\\bar{J} \\leq 0.104$ for helped, $\\leq 0.096$ for hurt, indicating ${\\sim}90$\\% non-overlap. Overlap significantly exceeds the independence baseline ($J_{\\text{null}} \\approx 0.05$, all $z > 5.9$, $p < .0001$; Appendix~\\ref{app:jaccard_permutation}), but normalizing by the theoretical upper bound $J_{\\max} = \\min(|A|,|B|)/\\max(|A|,|B|)$, observed overlap reaches only 5--7\\% of the achievable range above chance (normalized ES 0.065 helped, 0.062 hurt; per-pair details in Appendix~\\ref{app:normalized_es}), confirming that low Jaccard cannot be attributed to set-size constraints.",
          " ",
          " No surface-level feature (token count, word frequency, syntactic complexity) reliably separates helped from hurt examples. However, pure-model confidence on the target token is strongly predictive within each architecture: hurt examples have lower target-token probability among pure-correct examples ($d = 0.83$--$0.92$), and helped examples have higher probability among pure-wrong examples ($d = 0.73$--$0.77$); see Appendix~\\ref{app:confidence_margin}. A Kruskal-Wallis test confirms cross-architecture structure in both hurt ($p = .0005$) and helped ($p = .020$) distributions. This structured per-example disagreement despite similar aggregate performance constitutes predictive multiplicity \\citep{marx2020predictive} arising from architectural variation rather than random initialization \\citep{damour2022underspecification}.",
          " "
        ]
      }
```

```json
    "newString": "(\\S\\ref{ssec:per_example}, \\S\\ref{ssec:decoupling}, Figure~\\ref{fig:decoupling})",
    "originalFile": "\\section{Introduction}\n\nWhen attention layers are added to three different recurrent architectures, their LAMBADA performance becomes near-identical: cross-architecture standard deviation drops from 0.024 to 0.005, a 79\\% reduction. Yet this aggregate convergence hides an asymmetry at the per-example level. The three architectures reach similar overall accuracy by helping and hurting almost entirely different examples, with pairwise Jaccard similarity at most 0.11. Two models can agree on a benchmark score while disagreeing on which inputs they get right. This tension between aggregate agreement and per-example disagreement arises in the context of a rapid architectural shift toward hybrid recurrent-attention models, which interleave attention layers within linear recurrent backbones. Jamba \\citep{lieber2024jamba}, Griffin \\citep{de2024griffin}, RWKV-6 \\citep{peng2024eagle}, and RecurrentGemma \\citep{botev2024recurrentgemma} each combine recurrence with attention using different mixing strategies. These systems now serve as production-grade alternatives to pure Transformers, and the practical question of how much attention to include is typically answered by comparing aggregate benchmark numbers. \\citet{wang2025hybrid} provide a controlled model zoo spanning three recurrent architectures, five attention ratios, and two parameter scales, which enables systematic study of how attention allocation affects these hybrid designs.\n\nMost evaluations of hybrid architectures compare aggregate benchmark scores and report that a given attention ratio achieves a certain accuracy averaged across tasks or averaged across architectures. This practice can mask substantial per-example behavioral diversity. \\citet{marx2020predictive} formalized this risk as predictive multiplicity: a prediction problem admits competing models with conflicting predictions on individual inputs. \\citet{damour2022underspecification} documented a related phenomenon, underspecification, showing that models trained from the same pipeline with different random seeds can disagree on individual predictions despite equivalent aggregate performance. \\citet{mccoy2020berts} demonstrated a concrete NLP instance: BERT models trained from different random seeds diverge on specific linguistic constructions. These studies examine variation from initialization or training noise within a single architecture. Whether predictive multiplicity extends to hybrid recurrent architectures, where the source of variation is the recurrent backbone itself, remains unquantified. The question matters because practitioners increasingly choose between recurrent backbones based on aggregate benchmarks; if architectures that score identically in aggregate behave differently on individual inputs, such choices carry hidden consequences for deployment reliability and ensemble construction.\n\nWe present the first systematic per-example analysis of how attention affects cross-architecture agreement in hybrid recurrent models. Using the model zoo of \\citet{wang2025hybrid}, we compare three architectures (GatedDeltaNet, RetNet, HGRN2) at five attention ratios (0\\% to 25\\%) and two scales (340M and 1.3B parameters). These three architectures represent distinct approaches to linear recurrence: gated delta rules, multi-scale exponential decay, and gated state expansion. We evaluate all 30 configurations on 10 benchmarks spanning retrieval, comprehension, reasoning, and mixed task categories. On LAMBADA, we conduct a full per-example decomposition across 5{,}153 examples, where each example is classified as helped, hurt, or unchanged when attention is added to each architecture. We then measure cross-architecture overlap in these per-example effect sets using Jaccard similarity. Figure~\\ref{fig:decoupling} previews our central result: aggregate convergence and per-example divergence follow different trajectories as attention ratio increases.\n\n\\begin{figure}[t]\n\\centering\n\\includegraphics[width=\\columnwidth]{figures/fig1_decoupling_hero.pdf}\n\\caption{Aggregate convergence and per-example divergence are decoupled. (a) Cross-architecture standard deviation on LAMBADA decreases gradually with attention ratio at 1.3B (solid) but partially rebounds at 340M (dashed). (b) Pairwise Jaccard similarity of per-example helped/hurt sets saturates at the first attention level (4\\%) and remains flat.}\n\\label{fig:decoupling}\n\\end{figure}\n\nOur analysis yields three findings. First, attention-driven convergence is task-specific. Of 10 benchmarks, only LAMBADA exhibits monotonic reduction in cross-architecture variance ($\\rho = -1.0$, BCa 95\\% CI $[-1.0, -0.7]$; cross-architecture $\\sigma$ drops 79\\%). LAMBADA \\citep{paperno2016lambada} is a pure context-retrieval task that requires predicting the final word of a passage from long-range dependencies; attention provides direct token-level access to the full context, which offers a natural explanation for why convergence occurs on this task and not others. Benchmarks that involve reasoning or comprehension show no convergence, with all $|\\rho| \\leq 0.7$ and $p \\geq .233$. The Bonferroni-adjusted $p = .100$ does not survive strict correction at $\\alpha = 0.05$; the confidence interval, which excludes zero, provides a complementary characterization of the effect. Second, convergence depends on scale. At 1.3B parameters the $\\sigma$ trajectory is monotonically decreasing, but at 340M it is non-monotonic: $\\sigma$ partially rebounds at the highest attention ratio, with only 36\\% net reduction versus 79\\% at 1.3B. This contrast provides a preliminary observation that sufficient model capacity may facilitate complete equalization on retrieval. Third, and central to our title-level claim, aggregate convergence and per-example divergence are decoupled. Cross-architecture $\\sigma$ on LAMBADA decreases gradually across attention ratios ($0.024 \\to 0.016 \\to 0.014 \\to 0.006 \\to 0.005$), but pairwise Jaccard similarity of per-example helped and hurt sets saturates at the first attention level (4\\%) and remains flat at approximately 0.095. Within each architecture, pure-model confidence on the target token predicts which examples are helped or hurt ($d = 0.73$--$0.92$), but this predictability does not transfer across architectures. These two quantities operate on different timescales: aggregate performance equalizes progressively, while per-example specificity is established from the earliest attention allocation and does not change with more attention.\n\nOur contributions are as follows:\n\\begin{itemize}\n    \\item We quantify task-specific convergence: only retrieval tasks (LAMBADA) show attention-driven cross-architecture convergence at 1.3B scale; reasoning and comprehension benchmarks do not (\\S\\ref{ssec:task_convergence}, Table~\\ref{tab:convergence}, Figure~\\ref{fig:task_specificity}).\n    \\item We characterize scale-dependent convergence: the monotonic convergence pattern at 1.3B becomes non-monotonic at 340M, a preliminary observation that sufficient scale may facilitate complete equalization (\\S\\ref{ssec:scale_convergence}).\n    \\item We demonstrate that aggregate convergence and per-example divergence are decoupled: attention reduces cross-architecture variance gradually, but per-example overlap saturates at the first attention level and does not increase further, constituting a form of predictive multiplicity arising from architectural variation (\\S\\ref{ssec:decoupling}, Table~\\ref{tab:per_example}, Figure~\\ref{fig:decoupling}).\n\\end{itemize}\n",
```

```text
{"result":"{\"success\": true, \"data\": {\"path\": \"/home/ubuntu/.agent-ml-research-idea_gen_0509_2/projects/entrainment_gated_ft/paper_framing.md\", \"content\": \"# Paper Framing — entrainment_gated_ft\\n\\n> Revised 2026-05-19 after Dream review (best case = 2.5/5). Major reframing: drop \\\"paradox\\\" narrative, engage related work, add retrieval benchmarks.\\n\\n## Target Venue\\n- Primary: EMNLP 2026 Findings (via ARR)\\n- Stretch: EMNLP 2026 Main\\n- Deadline: ARR June 2026 cycle (submission ~June 15)\\n\\n## Paper Type\\nEmpirical analysis paper — quantifying a known phenomenon (per-example divergence under aggregate convergence) in a specific important domain (hybrid recurrent architectures)\\n\\n## Core Claims (3 条)\\n\\n### Claim 1: Task-Specific Convergence (revised D009)\\nAdding attention layers to different linear recurrent architectures (GatedDeltaNet, RetNet, HGRN2) induces aggregate performance convergence **specifically on context-retrieval tasks**. Across 10 benchmarks, only LAMBADA shows significant convergence (ρ=-1.0, p=0.017 two-tailed, cross-arch std drops 79%, BCa CI [-1.0, -0.7]). This task-specificity IS the finding, not a limitation:\\n- **Negative-ρ cluster** (6/10): LAMBADA, xstorycloze, COPA, PIQA, ARC-Easy, MMLU — tasks with context-retrieval or reasoning components\\n- **Positive-ρ cluster** (4/10): BoolQ, SciQ, HellaSwag, WinoGrande — commonsense/knowledge tasks where attention doesn't equalize\\n- LAMBADA is unique as a **pure context-prediction** task (predict last word from context alone), explaining why convergence is strongest there\\n\\n**Statistical strategy (D009)**:\\n1. **Primary evidence**: BCa percentile CI [-1.0, -0.7] (100% valid resamples have ρ<0; does NOT depend on multiple comparison correction)\\n2. **Pre-registered correction (D003)**: 6-benchmark Bonferroni adj-p=0.100. Report honestly as \\\"does not survive strict Bonferroni\\\"\\n3. **Supplementary**: 10-benchmark survey as task-specificity evidence, NOT for multi-comparison correction\\n4. ⚠️ **Prohibited**: post-hoc subset selection (\\\"2-benchmark Bonferroni\\\", \\\"only ρ<0 benchmarks\\\"). Reviewers will immediately flag this as p-hacking\\n\\n### Claim 2: Scale-Dependent Convergence\\nThe convergence effect requires sufficient model capacity: monotonically complete at 1.3B (ρ=-1.0, p=0.017 two-tailed) but only partial at 340M with a non-monotonic rebound (peak 56% reduction at h6-1, but endpoint only 36% due to rebound at h3-1; ρ=-0.5, p=0.45). Only two scale points (Wang et al. cap at 1.3B). Frame as binary contrast, not extrapolatable trend.\\n\\n**Cross-scale per-example stability (exp-007)**: Despite aggregate divergence across scales, per-example Jaccard at 340M h3-1 (helped=0.094, hurt=0.090) nearly matches 1.3B (~0.096, ~0.090). Net direction also consistent: GDN net negative, HGRN2 net positive at both scales (340M magnitudes larger: GDN=-256 vs -153, HGRN2=+238 vs +153). This extends the decoupling finding to the scale dimension: aggregate convergence is scale-dependent but per-example architecture-specificity is scale-invariant.\\n\\n### Claim 3: Per-Example Divergence Under Aggregate Convergence\\nDespite aggregate convergence, attention acts on nearly non-overlapping sample subsets per architecture (pairwise Jaccard < 0.10). This is an instance of the well-documented underspecification phenomenon (D'Amour et al. 2022) applied to hybrid architectures. Our contribution is **quantifying** it in this specific context, NOT claiming to discover it. Surface features AND baseline model perplexity do not differentiate helped/hurt samples within an architecture — the effect is opaque to standard features. However, cross-architecture comparison reveals systematic differences in baseline difficulty profiles of affected samples (Kruskal-Wallis p=0.0005 for hurt samples), confirming the architecture-specificity is structured, not random noise.\\n\\n**Decoupling finding (exp-010, D008)**: Aggregate convergence and per-example divergence are **decoupled** — they operate on different timescales w.r.t. attention ratio:\\n- Aggregate σ decreases **gradually** across 5 attention levels (0.024 → 0.016 → 0.014 → 0.006 → 0.005)\\n- Per-example Jaccard **saturates immediately** at the first attention level (4%) and remains flat (~0.095)\\n- Net asymmetry (HGRN2 net positive vs GDN/RetNet net negative) **increases** with attention (0.013 → 0.040)\\n- Implication: minimal attention creates full per-example architecture-specificity; gradual increases only affect aggregate convergence\\n\\n⚠️ Do NOT frame as \\\"co-evolution.\\\" Spearman ρ=-0.70 (p=0.23) is inflated by degenerate pure=0 point (Jaccard=NaN→0); excluding it: ρ=-0.40, p=0.75. The correct narrative is **decoupling**, which is a stronger finding: aggregate metrics converge while per-example dynamics increasingly diverge.\\n\\n## Story Spine\\n\\n### Problem\\nHybrid architectures mixing linear recurrence with full attention are becoming standard. While Wang et al. (2025) provide an extensive model zoo, the community lacks quantitative understanding of how attention affects cross-architecture behavioral diversity at the per-example level.\\n\\n### Related Work Positioning (CRITICAL — Dream W2)\\nThe observation that models with similar aggregate performance differ per-example is well-established:\\n- **Ensemble diversity**: Krogh & Vedelsby (1995), Kuncheva & Whitaker (2003) — diverse ensemble members err on different examples by construction\\n- **Underspecification**: D'Amour et al. (2022) — equivalent aggregate performance masks prediction-level differences\\n- **Dataset cartography**: Swayamdipta et al. (2020) — per-example difficulty varies across models\\n- **Simpson's paradox / ecological fallacy**: Aggregate-vs-disaggregate discrepancy is a textbook phenomenon\\n\\nOur contribution is NOT discovering this phenomenon. It IS quantifying how it manifests specifically when attention is added to linear recurrent models, and characterizing its task-dependency and scale-dependency. This is valuable because hybrid architectures are a design choice practitioners make — understanding per-example implications informs that choice.\\n\\n### Our Framing\\nWe provide the first systematic per-example analysis of attention's equalizing effect in hybrid recurrent architectures. Using Wang et al.'s public model zoo (72 models), we quantify (a) task-dependent convergence strength, (b) scale-dependent convergence completeness, and (c) architecture-specific per-example divergence patterns. Our contribution is analytical depth on public infrastructure, not new methods or models.\\n\\n### Headline Result Template\\n\\\"At 1.3B scale, adding attention to hybrid recurrent models reduces cross-architecture variance by 79% on LAMBADA, yet per-example analysis reveals nearly non-overlapping affected sample subsets (Jaccard < 0.10) — a manifestation of underspecification that has practical implications for architecture selection.\\\"\\n\\n## Dream Review Findings (D006) — Must Address\\n\\n### W1: Single-benchmark significance [MAJOR, mitigated via D009]\\nAfter Bonferroni (6 benchmarks), LAMBADA adj-p=0.100. exp-011 tested 5 additional benchmarks (boolq/copa/piqa/sciq/xstorycloze_en) — **none showed significant convergence**. Multi-benchmark path is closed.\\n**Mitigation (D009)**: (a) BCa CI [-1.0, -0.7] as primary evidence (doesn't depend on multiple comparison); (b) task-specificity reframing — LAMBADA's uniqueness as pure context-prediction task explains why it alone shows convergence; (c) 10-benchmark negative results presented as task-specificity evidence in supplementary; (d) honest reporting: \\\"does not survive Bonferroni\\\" in text. Report both.\\n6. **Effect sizes**: Cohen's d CI crosses zero; lead with BCa CI and convergence magnitude (std 0.024→0.005, 79% reduction).\\n\\n**Task taxonomy (must define in Method, NOT post-hoc)**:\\nOperationalize \\\"pure context retrieval\\\" as: next-token prediction requiring long-range context retrieval without additional reasoning/knowledge steps. LAMBADA fits (predict final word from narrative context). xstorycloze/COPA involve commonsense completion (multiple-choice, need world knowledge). BoolQ is reading comprehension (classify: \\\"retrieval\\\" → \\\"comprehension/QA\\\"). PIQA/SciQ involve domain knowledge. This taxonomy must appear in Method section before results.\\n\\n**Limitations to proactively address**:\\n- Per-example analysis limited to LAMBADA (only benchmark with significant convergence + available per-example logprob data). Acknowledge and explain. (D010: exp-002 cancelled — multi-benchmark per-example risks undermining task-specificity narrative)\\n- n=5 attention ratios limits statistical power (minimum achievable p = 1/120 ≈ 0.0083)\\n\\n### Concern C: \\\"The per-example divergence is a known phenomenon\\\"\\nResponse: Acknowledge explicitly. Our contribution is quantification in hybrid architectures, not discovery of the phenomenon. Cite ensemble diversity + underspecification.\\nRequired: Related Work section (Paper Agent).\\n\\n### Concern D: \\\"n=3 architectures is too few\\\"\\nResponse: 3 architectures × 5 ratios × 2 scales = 30 models. Per-example analysis provides 5153 data points per comparison. Check for additional architectures (exp-005).\\n\\n### Concern E: \\\"Two scales don't establish a trend\\\"\\nResponse: Binary contrast, not trend. 7B unavailable. Frame as \\\"scale is a necessary condition.\\\" exp-007 for 340M rebound mechanism.\\n\\n### Concern F: \\\"No causal mechanism\\\" (NEW from Dream)\\nResponse: We do NOT claim causal mechanism. We characterize and describe. Attention weight analysis (exp-004) is descriptive. Interventional experiments (head swaps, ablations) are acknowledged as future work. Honest framing prevents overclaiming.\\n\", \"total_chars\": 12090, \"truncated\": false}, \"error\": null, \"metadata\": {}}"
```

_source line 1859 · 2026-05-20T05:28:52.561Z_

<details><summary>↩ Tool result · `toolu_01SYhWKXTv6XW648L4CMuQqM`</summary>

```text
37	- Per-example Jaccard **saturates immediately** at the first attention level (4%) and remains flat (~0.095)
38	- Net asymmetry (HGRN2 net positive vs GDN/RetNet net negative) **increases** with attention (0.013 → 0.040)
39	- Implication: minimal attention creates full per-example architecture-specificity; gradual increases only affect aggregate convergence
40	
41	⚠️ Do NOT frame as "co-evolution." Spearman ρ=-0.70 (p=0.23) is inflated by degenerate pure=0 point (Jaccard=NaN→0); excluding it: ρ=-0.40, p=0.75. The correct narrative is **decoupling**, which is a stronger finding: aggregate metrics converge while per-example dynamics increasingly diverge.
```

</details>

<details><summary>↩ Tool result · `toolu_017pEQeLZjz4FguDs5xncxDa`</summary>

```text
28	The convergence effect requires sufficient model capacity: monotonically complete at 1.3B (ρ=-1.0, p=0.017 two-tailed) but only partial at 340M with a non-monotonic rebound (peak 56% reduction at h6-1, but endpoint only 36% due to rebound at h3-1; ρ=-0.5, p=0.45). Only two scale points (Wang et al. cap at 1.3B). Frame as binary contrast, not extrapolatable trend.
29	
30	**Cross-scale per-example stability (exp-007)**: Despite aggregate divergence across scales, per-example Jaccard at 340M h3-1 (helped=0.094, hurt=0.090) nearly matches 1.3B (~0.096, ~0.090). Net direction also consistent: GDN net negative, HGRN2 net positive at both scales (340M magnitudes larger: GDN=-256 vs -153, HGRN2=+238 vs +153). This extends the decoupling finding to the scale dimension: aggregate convergence is scale-dependent but per-example architecture-specificity is scale-invariant.
31	
32	### Claim 3: Per-Example Divergence Under Aggregate Convergence
```

```json
  "new_string": "### Claim 3: Per-Example Predictive Multiplicity Under Aggregate Convergence\nDespite aggregate convergence, attention acts on nearly non-overlapping sample subsets per architecture (pairwise Jaccard < 0.10, normalized ES ~6% of theoretical upper bound). This is an instance of **predictive multiplicity** (Marx et al. 2020) arising from architectural variation rather than random initialization (cf. D'Amour et al. 2022 underspecification, which is complementary but focuses on same-pipeline variation). Our contribution is **quantifying** it in hybrid recurrent architectures. RetNet-HGRN2 only analysis confirms decoupling holds without GDN confound (σ↓54%, J saturates 0.085-0.108)."
}
```

```json
  "content": "[审稿报告]\nOverall: 3.0\n\n# 审稿报告：Task-Specific Convergence and Per-Example Predictive Multiplicity in Hybrid Recurrent Architectures\n\n**Venue**: EMNLP 2026 via ARR June 2026（截止 2026-06-15）\n**Reviewer 编排**: 2 人（R1: 方法严谨性/统计分析; R2: 新颖性/related work/framing）\n**轮次**: Round 1（独立审稿 + 交叉回应，1 轮收敛）\n**收敛状态**: 关键问题达成广泛共识（14/16 条 AGREE），novelty 严重程度存在可记录分歧\n\n---\n\n## Summary\n\n论文对 hybrid recurrent architectures（GatedDeltaNet, RetNet, HGRN2）在不同 attention ratio（0%-25%）和 scale（340M, 1.3B）下的 cross-architecture performance convergence 进行了系统的 per-example 分析。使用 Wang et al. (2025) 的 controlled model zoo，在 10 个 benchmark 上评估 aggregate convergence，并在 LAMBADA 上进行 5,153 个样本的 per-example decomposition。核心发现：(1) 仅 LAMBADA 展示 cross-architecture convergence（ρ=−1.0，但 Bonferroni 后 p=.100）；(2) convergence 在 1.3B 单调，340M 非单调；(3) aggregate convergence 与 per-example divergence（Jaccard ≤ 0.11）解耦——前者随 attention ratio 递增，后者在 4% attention 时即饱和。\n\n---\n\n## Strengths\n\n1. **实验设计干净**: Controlled model zoo 排除训练数据和超参数差异的混淆，isolate 了 architecture 和 attention ratio 的效果（§3.1）。[R1, R2 共识]\n2. **统计报告透明度异常高**: 主动报告 Bonferroni 后不显著（p=.100）、bootstrap degenerate resamples（11.25%）、GDN head-count confound、normalized ES（5-7%）——这种透明度在 NLP 论文中罕见。[R1, R2 共识]\n3. **Per-example decomposition 分析视角有价值**: Helped/hurt/unchanged 分类 + Jaccard overlap + normalized effect size 是一套系统的分析工具。[R1, R2 共识]\n4. **Appendix robustness checks 充分**: GDN exclusion sensitivity、attention weight analysis、cross-scale Jaccard、permutation independence test、confidence margin analysis（Appendices B-K）。[R1, R2 共识]\n5. **Decoupling 发现有新意**: Aggregate convergence 和 per-example divergence 在不同时间尺度运作的发现，如果能在更多 benchmark 上验证，对 practitioners 有重要意义。[R1 肯定; R2 部分肯定]\n6. **可复现性高**: 基于公开 model zoo + lm-evaluation-harness 标准评估。[R1, R2 共识]\n\n---\n\n## Weaknesses\n\n### Must-Fix（影响接收决定）\n\n**N1** [critical | statistical_methodology] **统计功效天花板：设计层面不可能达到校正后显著性**\n\nn=5 attention ratios 的精确排列检验最小可达 p=2/120=0.0167；Bonferroni 校正 6 个 confirmatory benchmarks 后 ≥ 0.100。即使 ρ=−1.0（最极端结果），在实验设计层面也不可能达到 α=0.05。论文转向 n=3 架构 bootstrap CI 作为\"complementary characterization\"（§4.1），但该 CI 仅有 24 种非退化组合（3^3=27 中 3 种退化，占 11.1%），BCa 在边界值退化回退到 percentile CI，n=3 无渐近覆盖保证。p 值不显著时依赖 CI，实质上是 post-hoc 证据标准切换。[R1-N1, R1-N2; R2 [AGREE]]\n\n**N2** [critical | generalizability] **核心发现仅在 1/10 benchmarks 上成立，标题暗示更广适用性**\n\n10 个 benchmark 中仅 LAMBADA 表现出 monotonic convergence（ρ=−1.0），其余 9 个 |ρ| ≤ 0.7，p ≥ .233。Bonferroni 后 LAMBADA 也不显著（p=.100）。标题 \"Task-Specific Convergence\" 暗示按任务区分的系统性规律，实际是 1 个正例 + 9 个零结果。Decoupling claim（标题级发现）完全建立在 LAMBADA 这一个统计证据不充分的 benchmark 上——如果 convergence 不成立，decoupling 前提也不成立。[R1-N3, R2-N1; 双方交叉 [AGREE]]\n\n**N3** [critical | related_work] **遗漏关键相关工作**\n\n- **Fehlauer, Mahowald & Pimentel (EMNLP 2025)** \"Convergence and Divergence of LMs under Different Random Seeds\"——同 venue 前一年，直接研究 LM convergence/divergence，发现 larger models reconverge faster。R2 认为 must-cite + novelty 重叠；R1 认为 should-cite（不同 variation source：seed vs architecture），但**双方均同意应引用**。\n- **Sellam et al. (ICLR 2022)** MultiBERTs——25 个不同 seed 的 BERT 模型 + per-example robustness 分析。[R1, R2 共识 should-cite]\n\n---\n\n### Reviewer Consensus（AGREE）\n\n以下问题两位 Reviewer 在交叉回应中达成共识：\n\n**W1** [warning | confound] **GDN h3-1 的 16-head confound 应在正文讨论**\n\nGDN h3-1 在 1.3B 使用 16 attention heads（vs 其他 32）。移除 GDN 后 LAMBADA ρ 从 −1.0 降至 −0.7（p=.233），MMLU 方向反转。§4.5 RetNet-HGRN2 pair 部分缓解（σ 下降 54%），但 \"monotonic convergence at 1.3B\" 叙事不再完全成立。目前仅在 Appendix C 讨论。[R1-W1; R2 [AGREE] 并从 Minor 升级]\n\n**W2** [warning | overclaim] **Scale claim 过度推断**\n\n\"Scale as necessary condition\" 基于 2 个 data points（340M, 1.3B）。Introduction 用 \"We characterize\" 提升为 contribution，但 §4.2 自己承认 \"a preliminary observation from two data points\"。措辞不一致。[R1-W4, R2-W1; 双方 [AGREE]]\n\n**W3** [warning | framing] **标题 scope 过大**\n\n\"Hybrid Recurrent Architectures\" 暗示普适覆盖，实际仅 3 个架构 + 1 个核心 benchmark。R1 建议 \"...Evidence from LAMBADA\"；R2 建议 \"A Case Study of Three...\"。[R2-W3; R1 [AGREE with nuance]]\n\n**W4** [warning | methodology] **Predictive Multiplicity 框架应用不严格**\n\nJaccard similarity 是 overlap proxy，不是 formal multiplicity metric。至少应计算 ambiguity（Marx et al. 2020: fraction of examples where models disagree）或 discrepancy。标题写入 \"Predictive Multiplicity\" 但未使用任何 formal metric。[R2-W4; R1 [AGREE]]\n\n**W5** [warning | experimental_validity] **Jaccard \"nearly disjoint\" 表述过度**\n\nJ_obs ≈ 0.10 仅为 J_null ≈ 0.05 的 2 倍；normalized ES 仅 5-7% of theoretical maximum。\"Nearly disjoint\" 过度简化——更准确是 \"low overlap, approximately twice independence baseline\"。[R1-W3; R2 [AGREE]]\n\n**W6** [warning | impact] **使用他人 model zoo 的贡献边界**\n\n无新训练、新数据、干预实验。纯分析贡献需更强统计证据或更广实证覆盖来补偿。[R1-W2; R2 [AGREE]]\n\n**W7** [warning | overclaim] **Attention weight analysis 结论过强**\n\nCosine similarity、entropy、last-5 attention mass 均为 null results。\"Per-example effects originate primarily in recurrent layers\"（§4.4）是排除法推断，无直接 recurrent layer 分析。应改为 \"are not explained by attention layers, consistent with originating in...\"。[R1-W5; R2 [AGREE]]\n\n---\n\n### Reviewer Divergence（分歧）\n\n**N4** [R2: critical; R1: should-fix | novelty] **新颖性评估分歧**\n\n- **R2 观点**: 论文本质上是 D'Amour et al. (2022) underspecification 在 hybrid recurrent 设定下的验证，使用他人 model zoo + 标准方法，无方法论增量。Predictive multiplicity 写入标题但未使用 formal metric。构成 critical 问题。\n- **R1 反驳**: (1) Variation source 是 architectural backbone 而非 random seed，不是 underspecification 的复现；(2) Dataset Cartography (Swayamdipta et al., 2020, EMNLP) 是纯观察性分析被接收的先例；(3) 但同意 formal multiplicity metric 缺失是 should-fix。\n- **Director 判断**: 两种评估均有合理依据。R2 的 critical 评级基于更严格的增量标准；R1 的 should-fix 基于分析论文先例。记录分歧供 Research Director 判断。\n\n---\n\n## Questions\n\n1. 是否考虑 FDR 控制（Benjamini-Hochberg）替代 Bonferroni？BH 校正下 LAMBADA 的 adjusted p ≈ 0.017 可能通过 α=0.05。\n2. 对 ARC-Easy（ρ=−0.7）和 MMLU（ρ=−0.7）能否做类似 LAMBADA 的 per-example 分析？即使 convergence 不显著，Jaccard saturation 的测试仍有价值——如果在非 convergence benchmark 上也观察到类似的 per-example overlap 模式，能提供有意义的对照。\n3. GDN confidence margin 分析为何使用 h12-1 而非 h3-1（Appendix K）？如果因 16-head confound，请在正文说明。\n4. 是否有计划公开分析代码和 per-example 分类数据？\n\n---\n\n## Missing References\n\n| 文献 | 相关性 | 审稿人共识 |\n|------|--------|-----------|\n| Fehlauer, Mahowald & Pimentel (EMNLP 2025) \"Convergence and Divergence of LMs under Different Random Seeds\" | 高度相关——同 venue 前一年 | R2: must-cite; R1: should-cite |\n| Sellam et al. (ICLR 2022) \"The MultiBERTs\" | 相关——per-example robustness across seeds | R1, R2: should-cite |\n| Bui et al. (IJCNLP-AACL 2025) \"Macro and Micro Effects of Random Seeds\" | 边缘相关 | R2: can-cite |\n\n---\n\n## External Review (Stanford Agentic Reviewer)\n\n外部审稿：未请求（项目未配置 external_review）。\n\n---\n\n## Visual Layout Check（非评分项）\n\n视觉审查已完成。发现摘要：\n\n- **Critical（1）**: 末页（p.15）约 75% 为空白，仅一张表格占底部——视觉上不专业，应重新排布\n- **Warning（4）**:\n  - pp.3-5 连续 3 页纯文本（Related Work + Method），无任何 display item（figure/table/equation）——审稿人易失去注意力\n  - 主正文 8 页仅 3 个 display items（Figure 1, Figure 2, Table 1），远低于实证论文常见密度（5-8/页）；核心发现以文字呈现而 Appendix 堆积 14 张表\n  - Method 部分核心定义（σ reduction, Jaccard, convergence ratio）仅以 inline math 呈现，缺少 numbered display equations\n  - Conclusion + Limitations 页纯文本无 visual element\n- **Info（6）**: Figure 1 位置良好但 axis label 偏小；Figure 2 受限单栏；Table 1 格式规范；Appendix 表格密度高但可读；References 格式统一无错误\n\n---\n\n## Overall Assessment\n\n**Overall Score: 3.0（Findings）**\n\n| 维度 | R1 | R2 | 综合 |\n|------|----|----|------|\n| Soundness | 3.0 | 3.0 | 3.0 |\n| Excitement | 2.5 | 2.5 | 2.5 |\n| Overall | 3.0 | 2.5 | 3.0 |\n| Confidence | 4/5 | 4/5 | 高 |\n| Reproducibility | 4/5 | 4/5 | 4/5 |\n\n**综合评价**:\n\n论文提出了一个有价值的研究问题（architectural predictive multiplicity），分析方法系统且统计报告透明度异常高。Decoupling 发现——aggregate convergence 和 per-example divergence 的时间尺度分离——在概念上是论文最有趣的贡献。\n\n但三个核心问题严重限制了当前版本的 impact：\n\n1. **统计功效先天不足**（n=5 + Bonferroni = 设计层面不可能显著），且 CI 替代证据基于 n=3 bootstrap，形成两端不牢靠的证据链\n2. **Single-benchmark scope**: 标题级 claim 建立在单一 benchmark（LAMBADA）的非显著统计结果上\n3. **GDN confound**: 移除有混淆变量的架构后核心结论显著弱化（ρ: −1.0 → −0.7）\n\n论文更适合 **Findings track**。统计报告的诚实和分析的系统性值得肯定，但 scope 过窄和统计局限性限制了 conference-level impact。\n\n**Rebuttal 提升路径**（如全部解决，评分可望提升至 3.5 Borderline Conference）：\n- (a) 扩展 per-example 分析到 ARC-Easy/MMLU 以测试 decoupling 泛化性\n- (b) 引入 formal multiplicity metric（ambiguity/discrepancy）\n- (c) 采用 BH 替代 Bonferroni\n- (d) 补引 Fehlauer et al. (EMNLP 2025) 并明确 differentiate\n- (e) 标题加 scope qualifier\n\n---\n\n## Issue Tracker 状态\n\n已同步更新 `review_issues.yaml`（15 个 issue）：\n- Critical: 3（stat power, single-benchmark scope, missing refs）\n- Major: 1（novelty — Reviewer 分歧）\n- Warning: 11（GDN confound, scale overclaim, title scope, multiplicity formal, Jaccard wording, contribution boundary, attention overreach, practical impact, pre-registration, entropy, attn-overreach）\n- 上轮已修复: 2 个 issue 本轮未被提及（W-preregistration, W-entropy），标记为可能已修复\n- 新增: 3 个 issue（W-attn-overreach, W-contribution-boundary, W-multiplicity-formal）\n---\n\n## ⚠️ 处理协议(强制,逐条执行,禁止简略)\n\n**收到上面的审稿报告后必须按以下顺序处理**:\n\n1. **逐条编号通读**: 把报告里所有问题、修改建议一项一项列出来(R1, R2, R3...),\n   即使有的看起来重复或次要也要单独保留 — 不允许漏任何一条。\n\n2. **每条给出详细修复方案**:\n   - 补实验: 写明实验配置(baseline / 超参 / seed / dataset / metric) + 预估 GPU 时长\n   - 改论文: 写明改哪一节、改写思路、新引用是否需要补\n   - 改 framing / 措辞: 写明原文 + 改写后版本\n   - 写 rebuttal: 写明论点和支撑证据\n   - 拒绝: 写明拒绝理由(reviewer 误读 / 超出 scope / 已有反驳证据)\n\n3. **必须调 `post_to_reviewer` 让 Research Reviewer 补充意见**:\n   把完整审稿报告 + 你的逐条初步方案打包发给 Reviewer, 让 ta 独立审视有没有\n   遗漏、方案可行性、技术细节是否合理。**这一步不可省** — Reviewer 是你的\n   独立审查员, 他对每条审稿意见的判断和你互补。\n\n4. **收到 Reviewer 补充后综合定稿**: 把 reviewer 反馈合并进你的 Action Matrix,\n   再安排 Main Agent 执行(补实验 / 改论文)或向用户确认 venue 变更。\n\n5. **同步 Issue Tracker**: Action Matrix 定稿后, 用 `read_local_file` 读\n   `projects/{project}/artifacts/review_issues.yaml`, 把本轮处理的 issue 标\n   `in_progress`、Reviewer 已确认修复的标 `resolved`, 调 `update_review_issues`\n   写回。这是跨轮次的修复追踪 — Continue 模式审稿要靠这个表识别复发问题。\n\n**禁止**:\n- 跳过第 3 步直接派 Main Agent\n- 把多条审稿意见合并成一条 action\n- 对低 priority 意见用\"暂不处理\"一笔带过而不给出明确理由\n- 整体性\"已知悉\"回复\n"
```

Overall: 3.0

# 审稿报告：Task-Specific Convergence and Per-Example Predictive Multiplicity in Hybrid Recurrent Architectures

**Venue**: EMNLP 2026 via ARR June 2026（截止 2026-06-15）
**Reviewer 编排**: 2 人（R1: 方法严谨性/统计分析; R2: 新颖性/related work/framing）
**轮次**: Round 1（独立审稿 + 交叉回应，1 轮收敛）
**收敛状态**: 关键问题达成广泛共识（14/16 条 AGREE），novelty 严重程度存在可记录分歧

---

## Summary

论文对 hybrid recurrent architectures（GatedDeltaNet, RetNet, HGRN2）在不同 attention ratio（0%-25%）和 scale（340M, 1.3B）下的 cross-architecture performance convergence 进行了系统的 per-example 分析。使用 Wang et al. (2025) 的 controlled model zoo，在 10 个 benchmark 上评估 aggregate convergence，并在 LAMBADA 上进行 5,153 个样本的 per-example decomposition。核心发现：(1) 仅 LAMBADA 展示 cross-architecture convergence（ρ=−1.0，但 Bonferroni 后 p=.100）；(2) convergence 在 1.3B 单调，340M 非单调；(3) aggregate convergence 与 per-example divergence（Jaccard ≤ 0.11）解耦——前者随 attention ratio 递增，后者在 4% attention 时即饱和。

---

## Strengths

1. **实验设计干净**: Controlled model zoo 排除训练数据和超参数差异的混淆，isolate 了 architecture 和 attention ratio 的效果（§3.1）。[R1, R2 共识]
2. **统计报告透明度异常高**: 主动报告 Bonferroni 后不显著（p=.100）、bootstrap degenerate resamples（11.25%）、GDN head-count confound、normalized ES（5-7%）——这种透明度在 NLP 论文中罕见。[R1, R2 共识]
3. **Per-example decomposition 分析视角有价值**: Helped/hurt/unchanged 分类 + Jaccard overlap + normalized effect size 是一套系统的分析工具。[R1, R2 共识]
4. **Appendix robustness checks 充分**: GDN exclusion sensitivity、attention weight analysis、cross-scale Jaccard、permutation independence test、confidence margin analysis（Appendices B-K）。[R1, R2 共识]
5. **Decoupling 发现有新意**: Aggregate convergence 和 per-example divergence 在不同时间尺度运作的发现，如果能在更多 benchmark 上验证，对 practitioners 有重要意义。[R1 肯定; R2 部分肯定]
6. **可复现性高**: 基于公开 model zoo + lm-evaluation-harness 标准评估。[R1, R2 共识]

---

## Weaknesses

### Must-Fix（影响接收决定）

**N1** [critical | statistical_methodology] **统计功效天花板：设计层面不可能达到校正后显著性**

n=5 attention ratios 的精确排列检验最小可达 p=2/120=0.0167；Bonferroni 校正 6 个 confirmatory benchmarks 后 ≥ 0.100。即使 ρ=−1.0（最极端结果），在实验设计层面也不可能达到 α=0.05。论文转向 n=3 架构 bootstrap CI 作为"complementary characterization"（§4.1），但该 CI 仅有 24 种非退化组合（3^3=27 中 3 种退化，占 11.1%），BCa 在边界值退化回退到 percentile CI，n=3 无渐近覆盖保证。p 值不显著时依赖 CI，实质上是 post-hoc 证据标准切换。[R1-N1, R1-N2; R2 [AGREE]]

**N2** [critical | generalizability] **核心发现仅在 1/10 benchmarks 上成立，标题暗示更广适用性**

10 个 benchmark 中仅 LAMBADA 表现出 monotonic convergence（ρ=−1.0），其余 9 个 |ρ| ≤ 0.7，p ≥ .233。Bonferroni 后 LAMBADA 也不显著（p=.100）。标题 "Task-Specific Convergence" 暗示按任务区分的系统性规律，实际是 1 个正例 + 9 个零结果。Decoupling claim（标题级发现）完全建立在 LAMBADA 这一个统计证据不充分的 benchmark 上——如果 convergence 不成立，decoupling 前提也不成立。[R1-N3, R2-N1; 双方交叉 [AGREE]]

**N3** [critical | related_work] **遗漏关键相关工作**

- **Fehlauer, Mahowald & Pimentel (EMNLP 2025)** "Convergence and Divergence of LMs under Different Random Seeds"——同 venue 前一年，直接研究 LM convergence/divergence，发现 larger models reconverge faster。R2 认为 must-cite + novelty 重叠；R1 认为 should-cite（不同 variation source：seed vs architecture），但**双方均同意应引用**。
- **Sellam et al. (ICLR 2022)** MultiBERTs——25 个不同 seed 的 BERT 模型 + per-example robustness 分析。[R1, R2 共识 should-cite]

---

### Reviewer Consensus（AGREE）

以下问题两位 Reviewer 在交叉回应中达成共识：

**W1** [warning | confound] **GDN h3-1 的 16-head confound 应在正文讨论**

GDN h3-1 在 1.3B 使用 16 attention heads（vs 其他 32）。移除 GDN 后 LAMBADA ρ 从 −1.0 降至 −0.7（p=.233），MMLU 方向反转。§4.5 RetNet-HGRN2 pair 部分缓解（σ 下降 54%），但 "monotonic convergence at 1.3B" 叙事不再完全成立。目前仅在 Appendix C 讨论。[R1-W1; R2 [AGREE] 并从 Minor 升级]

**W2** [warning | overclaim] **Scale claim 过度推断**

"Scale as necessary condition" 基于 2 个 data points（340M, 1.3B）。Introduction 用 "We characterize" 提升为 contribution，但 §4.2 自己承认 "a preliminary observation from two data points"。措辞不一致。[R1-W4, R2-W1; 双方 [AGREE]]

**W3** [warning | framing] **标题 scope 过大**

"Hybrid Recurrent Architectures" 暗示普适覆盖，实际仅 3 个架构 + 1 个核心 benchmark。R1 建议 "...Evidence from LAMBADA"；R2 建议 "A Case Study of Three..."。[R2-W3; R1 [AGREE with nuance]]

**W4** [warning | methodology] **Predictive Multiplicity 框架应用不严格**

Jaccard similarity 是 overlap proxy，不是 formal multiplicity metric。至少应计算 ambiguity（Marx et al. 2020: fraction of examples where models disagree）或 discrepancy。标题写入 "Predictive Multiplicity" 但未使用任何 formal metric。[R2-W4; R1 [AGREE]]

**W5** [warning | experimental_validity] **Jaccard "nearly disjoint" 表述过度**

J_obs ≈ 0.10 仅为 J_null ≈ 0.05 的 2 倍；normalized ES 仅 5-7% of theoretical maximum。"Nearly disjoint" 过度简化——更准确是 "low overlap, approximately twice independence baseline"。[R1-W3; R2 [AGREE]]

**W6** [warning | impact] **使用他人 model zoo 的贡献边界**

无新训练、新数据、干预实验。纯分析贡献需更强统计证据或更广实证覆盖来补偿。[R1-W2; R2 [AGREE]]

**W7** [warning | overclaim] **Attention weight analysis 结论过强**

Cosine similarity、entropy、last-5 attention mass 均为 null results。"Per-example effects originate primarily in recurrent layers"（§4.4）是排除法推断，无直接 recurrent layer 分析。应改为 "are not explained by attention layers, consistent with originating in..."。[R1-W5; R2 [AGREE]]

---

### Reviewer Divergence（分歧）

**N4** [R2: critical; R1: should-fix | novelty] **新颖性评估分歧**

- **R2 观点**: 论文本质上是 D'Amour et al. (2022) underspecification 在 hybrid recurrent 设定下的验证，使用他人 model zoo + 标准方法，无方法论增量。Predictive multiplicity 写入标题但未使用 formal metric。构成 critical 问题。
- **R1 反驳**: (1) Variation source 是 architectural backbone 而非 random seed，不是 underspecification 的复现；(2) Dataset Cartography (Swayamdipta et al., 2020, EMNLP) 是纯观察性分析被接收的先例；(3) 但同意 formal multiplicity metric 缺失是 should-fix。
- **Director 判断**: 两种评估均有合理依据。R2 的 critical 评级基于更严格的增量标准；R1 的 should-fix 基于分析论文先例。记录分歧供 Research Director 判断。

---

## Questions

1. 是否考虑 FDR 控制（Benjamini-Hochberg）替代 Bonferroni？BH 校正下 LAMBADA 的 adjusted p ≈ 0.017 可能通过 α=0.05。
2. 对 ARC-Easy（ρ=−0.7）和 MMLU（ρ=−0.7）能否做类似 LAMBADA 的 per-example 分析？即使 convergence 不显著，Jaccard saturation 的测试仍有价值——如果在非 convergence benchmark 上也观察到类似的 per-example overlap 模式，能提供有意义的对照。
3. GDN confidence margin 分析为何使用 h12-1 而非 h3-1（Appendix K）？如果因 16-head confound，请在正文说明。
4. 是否有计划公开分析代码和 per-example 分类数据？

---

## Overall Assessment

**Overall Score: 3.0（Findings）**

| 维度 | R1 | R2 | 综合 |
|------|----|----|------|
| Soundness | 3.0 | 3.0 | 3.0 |
| Excitement | 2.5 | 2.5 | 2.5 |
| Overall | 3.0 | 2.5 | 3.0 |
| Confidence | 4/5 | 4/5 | 高 |
| Reproducibility | 4/5 | 4/5 | 4/5 |

**综合评价**:

论文提出了一个有价值的研究问题（architectural predictive multiplicity），分析方法系统且统计报告透明度异常高。Decoupling 发现——aggregate convergence 和 per-example divergence 的时间尺度分离——在概念上是论文最有趣的贡献。

但三个核心问题严重限制了当前版本的 impact：

1. **统计功效先天不足**（n=5 + Bonferroni = 设计层面不可能显著），且 CI 替代证据基于 n=3 bootstrap，形成两端不牢靠的证据链
2. **Single-benchmark scope**: 标题级 claim 建立在单一 benchmark（LAMBADA）的非显著统计结果上
3. **GDN confound**: 移除有混淆变量的架构后核心结论显著弱化（ρ: −1.0 → −0.7）

论文更适合 **Findings track**。统计报告的诚实和分析的系统性值得肯定，但 scope 过窄和统计局限性限制了 conference-level impact。

**Rebuttal 提升路径**（如全部解决，评分可望提升至 3.5 Borderline Conference）：
- (a) 扩展 per-example 分析到 ARC-Easy/MMLU 以测试 decoupling 泛化性
- (b) 引入 formal multiplicity metric（ambiguity/discrepancy）
- (c) 采用 BH 替代 Bonferroni
- (d) 补引 Fehlauer et al. (EMNLP 2025) 并明确 differentiate
- (e) 标题加 scope qualifier

## ⚠️ 处理协议(强制,逐条执行,禁止简略)

**收到上面的审稿报告后必须按以下顺序处理**:

1. **逐条编号通读**: 把报告里所有问题、修改建议一项一项列出来(R1, R2, R3...),
   即使有的看起来重复或次要也要单独保留 — 不允许漏任何一条。

2. **每条给出详细修复方案**:
   - 补实验: 写明实验配置(baseline / 超参 / seed / dataset / metric) + 预估 GPU 时长
   - 改论文: 写明改哪一节、改写思路、新引用是否需要补
   - 改 framing / 措辞: 写明原文 + 改写后版本
   - 写 rebuttal: 写明论点和支撑证据
   - 拒绝: 写明拒绝理由(reviewer 误读 / 超出 scope / 已有反驳证据)

3. **必须调 `post_to_reviewer` 让 Research Reviewer 补充意见**:
   把完整审稿报告 + 你的逐条初步方案打包发给 Reviewer, 让 ta 独立审视有没有
   遗漏、方案可行性、技术细节是否合理。**这一步不可省** — Reviewer 是你的
   独立审查员, 他对每条审稿意见的判断和你互补。

4. **收到 Reviewer 补充后综合定稿**: 把 reviewer 反馈合并进你的 Action Matrix,
   再安排 Main Agent 执行(补实验 / 改论文)或向用户确认 venue 变更。

5. **同步 Issue Tracker**: Action Matrix 定稿后, 用 `read_local_file` 读
   `projects/{project}/artifacts/review_issues.yaml`, 把本轮处理的 issue 标
   `in_progress`、Reviewer 已确认修复的标 `resolved`, 调 `update_review_issues`
   写回。这是跨轮次的修复追踪 — Continue 模式审稿要靠这个表识别复发问题。

**禁止**:
- 跳过第 3 步直接派 Main Agent
- 把多条审稿意见合并成一条 action
- 对低 priority 意见用"暂不处理"一笔带过而不给出明确理由
- 整体性"已知悉"回复

```text
1	---
2	name: review-report-action
3	description: '当 Research Director 收到 Review 阶段回传的审稿报告时,立即使用本 skill。先做 Holistic Pass(识别 SYSTEMIC_ISSUE),再做 Per-Comment Action Matrix。禁止"已知悉"整体回复。'
4	always: false
5	roles:
6	  - director
7	---
8	
9	# Review Report Action Matrix
10	
11	## 硬约束
12	收到审稿报告后**禁止**整体性 "已知悉" 回复。必须**先做 Holistic Pass,再做 Per-Comment Action Matrix**。
13	
14	## Step 1: Holistic Pass（整体审视，action matrix 之前必做）
15	
16	审稿意见不是独立的,经常多条指向同一个系统性问题。切碎成多条独立 action 会漏掉根因。
17	
18	1. **聚类**：把所有审稿意见按主题聚类（method design / experiment design / claim strength / writing clarity / novelty / related work）
19	2. **识别系统性问题**：任何一个 cluster 含 ≥ 3 条意见 → 标记为 `[SYSTEMIC_ISSUE]`,不再切碎成独立 action,作为单独根因处理
20	3. **矩阵回溯**：如果 SYSTEMIC_ISSUE 指向 core method design → 立即触发 `experiment-matrix` skill 回顾:
21	   - 现有实验矩阵在新的 method 理解下还有几格有效？
22	   - 哪些之前的 positive 实验可能因此失效（method 基础变了）？
23	   - 哪些格子要重新跑？
24	4. **全局影响评估**：SYSTEMIC_ISSUE 是否影响论文 core claim？如果是 → 必须子调 `red-section` 做 framing 评估,然后再动 action matrix
25	
26	**Holistic Pass 输出写入 ROADMAP.md 的"审稿回溯·系统性问题"section**,先于 action matrix。
27	
28	## Step 2: Action Matrix 格式
29	
30	| 审稿意见 ID | 内容摘要 | 类别 | 动作 | 负责人 | ETA |
31	|-----------|--------|------|------|-------|-----|
32	| R1.1 | 缺某 ablation | 补实验 | 让 Agent 铺 exp_XXX | Agent | 2d |
33	| R1.2 | Claim 过强 | 改 paper | 调 red-section 重写 intro | Agent | 1d |
34	| R1.3 | 某 method 解释不清 | 写 rebuttal | Director 写 rebuttal 论点 | Director | 1d |
35	| R2.1 | Core claim 不成立 | framing 调整 | 子调 red-section + 改矩阵 | Agent + Director | 5d |
36	
37	## 类别
38	- **补实验**：必须让 Agent 铺实验（可与 rebuttal 并行）
39	- **改 paper**：让 Agent 调 red-section 后改章节
40	- **写 rebuttal**：Director 主笔
41	- **拒绝**：有理由的拒绝（写入 action matrix 的"拒绝理由"字段）
42	
43	## 产出
44	- action matrix 写入 ROADMAP.md 的"审稿回溯"section
45	- 决策日志同步（`log_decision`）
46	- 补实验条目立即调度（不等 rebuttal 写完）
47	- 如果有 "framing 调整" 类别 → 子调 red-section skill
48	
49	## Step 3: 同步 Issue Tracker（必做）
50	
```

_source line 1888 · 2026-05-20T06:00:32.622Z_

## Holistic Pass

**Cluster 1: 证据范围不足** (N2 + W3 + W6 = 3条) → **[SYSTEMIC_ISSUE]**
- N2: 仅 1/10 benchmark
- W3: 标题 scope 仍过大
- W6: 无新训练/干预，纯分析贡献需更广覆盖补偿
- **根因**：证据基仅 LAMBADA 一个 benchmark 的 per-example 分析，扩展证据范围是唯一突破口

**Cluster 2: 方法论精度** (W1 + W5 + W7 = 3条) → **[SYSTEMIC_ISSUE]**
- W1: GDN confound 仍需正文讨论
- W5: "nearly disjoint" 措辞过度
- W7: Attention overreach
- **根因**：措辞精度不够，需全面降级过度表述

**Cluster 3: 统计设计** (N1 + W2 = 2条)
- 设计层面不可解决（n=5 + n=3 是 model zoo 固有限制）

**Cluster 4: 新颖性/框架** (N4 + W4 = 2条)
- W4 是可操作的：计算 formal multiplicity metrics

**关键判断**：Round 1→Round 2 分数未动的原因不是写作，是**证据基太窄**。唯一能实质提分的是扩展 per-example 分析到 ARC-Easy/MMLU（Q2），以及补充 formal multiplicity metrics（W4）。

(2020) 实现 ambiguity 和 discrepancy：\n  - Ambiguity(x) = 1 if 至少 2 个 architecture 在 example x 上不同意\n  - Overall ambiguity = fraction of examples with disagreement\n  - 对每个 attention ratio 计算，看 ambiguity 如何随 attention 变化\n- 可以在 LAMBADA + ARC-Easy/MMLU 上都做\n- 预估：纯计算 <0.5 天\n- **直接回应 W4 \"标题写了 PM 但没用 formal metric\"**\n\n**A3: BH 校正分析** [计算, 已完成]\n- BH adjusted p = 0.102，与 Bonferroni 相同。因为只有 1/6 benchmark 有小 p，BH 无法改善\n- **论文修改**：加一句 \"We also considered FDR control (Benjamini-Hochberg); the adjusted p-value (0.102) does not differ from the Bonferroni correction, as no other benchmark approaches significance.\"\n- **rebuttal 回应 Q1**\n\n### 中优先级（论文修改）\n\n**A4: 补引 Fehlauer et al. [Reviewer 回复]:
[severity: warning] Round 2 Action Matrix 独立审查

---

## 验证回复

### V1: ARC-Easy/MMLU per-example 数据 — 不可用，需重跑

服务器上搜索确认：
- `find log_samples -type d` = 空
- `find *per_example*` = 仅 LAMBADA 的 2 个 csv
- 所有 results.json 仅含 aggregate scores（acc, perplexity, stderr）

**需要重跑 eval with `--log_samples` flag**。预估工作量：
- 1.3B: 15 模型 × 2 benchmarks（ARC-Easy 2376 examples, MMLU ~14k examples）
- 每个 eval job ~15-30 min（含 log_samples 输出）
- 可并行，但只有 1 GPU → 串行 ~8-15 小时
- 340M 如果也做：额外 15 模型 × 2 benchmarks

建议：先只跑 1.3B ARC-Easy（2376 examples, 15 models, ~4-6 小时），如果 pattern 有价值再扩展 MMLU 和 340M。

### V2: Fehlauer et al. (EMNLP 2025) — ✓ 真实

"Convergence and Divergence of Language Models under Different Random Seeds"
Fehlauer, Mahowald, Pimentel. EMNLP 2025 Main. arXiv:2509.26643, ACL Anthology: 2025.emnlp-main.1675.
研究 same-architecture different-seed 的 convergence，发现四阶段 pattern + model size 影响 reconvergence。
**与我们的差异化清晰**：他们 same-arch different-seed，我们 cross-arch same-training。

### V3: Sellam et al. (ICLR 2022) — ✓ 真实

"The MultiBERTs: BERT Reproductions for Robustness Analysis"
Sellam, Yadlowsky, Tenney, Wei, Saphra, D'Amour, Linzen 等. ICLR 2022 Spotlight. arXiv:2106.16163.
25 个 BERT checkpoints，Multi-Bootstrap 统计方法。
**关键**：Alexander D'Amour 是共同作者——同时参与了 underspecification 和 MultiBERTs，完美的引用桥梁。

---

## V4: Round 1 为什么没提分？

**我的独立判断：根因是证据基未扩展，不是写作问题。**

Round 1 的所有修改都是**防御性的**——修补已有弱点（floor effect → normalized ES, GDN confound → RetNet-HGRN2, CI framing → complementary）。这些防止扣分但不创造加分。

更严重的是，**标题改为 "Predictive Multiplicity" 反而造成了新的 expectation gap**：
- 审稿人现在期待 Marx 式的 formal metrics（ambiguity, discrepancy）→ 论文没有 → 新缺陷 (W4)
- 标题升级了 claim 范围但证据没变 → systemic issue #3 未解决

Round 1 的分数分布：一部分修复了 Round 1 扣分项（+），另一部分引入了新期望（-），净效果 ≈ 0。

---

## V5: 3.0→3.5 可行性评估

**条件估算**：

| 场景 | 预期分数 | 条件 |
|-------|---------|------|
| 仅论文措辞修改（A3-A8） | 3.0-3.1 | 不够 |
| + A2 formal metrics（LAMBADA only） | 3.1-3.2 | 回应 W4 但不扩展证据基 |
| + A1 ARC-Easy per-example（pattern similar to LAMBADA） | **3.3-3.5** | 扩展证据基 + 最强提分杠杆 |
| + A1 ARC-Easy per-example（pattern different） | 3.1-3.3 | 有价值的对照但不 compelling |

**3.5 需要 A1 产出正面结果**。最有价值的发现是：ARC-Easy 上也有低 cross-arch Jaccard（per-example divergence is universal），即使没有 aggregate convergence。这会把论文从"LAMBADA 上的观察"升级为"per-example divergence 是普遍现象，aggregate convergence 是例外"。

---

## 策略评估

### A1 是否值得做？— **值得，这是唯一能实质提分的项目**

但我要指出一个更锐利的 hypothesis framing：

**不要只是"扩展到 ARC-Easy"。核心 hypothesis 应该是：per-example divergence（低 Jaccard）在所有 benchmark 上都存在，不仅限于有 aggregate convergence 的 LAMBADA。** 如果成立，论文的 narrative 升级为：

"Predictive multiplicity is universal across task types. What is task-specific is not the per-example divergence (which is always present) but the aggregate convergence (which occurs only on retrieval tasks)."

这比"LAMBADA 上有 decoupling"强很多倍。即使 ARC-Easy 上 Jaccard 略高（比如 0.15 而非 0.10），只要远低于 J_max，论点就成立。

### A2 值得做但不是优先

A2 on LAMBADA alone 解决 W4 但对分数影响有限。建议：等 A1 的 ARC-Easy 数据出来后，A2 同时在 LAMBADA + ARC-Easy 上做 formal metrics。这样一次性解决 W4 + 扩展证据基。

### A5 标题建议

当前标题"Task-Specific Convergence and Per-Example Predictive Multiplicity in Hybrid Recurrent Architectures"——如果 A1 成功，可以保留不变（evidence 支撑了）。如果 A1 不做或失败，改为 "...A Case Study on LAMBADA" 是必要的。

### A6 措辞修改是低成本高回报

W5 "nearly disjoint" 和 W7 "originate primarily" 是每个审稿人都会注意到的过度措辞。改了不加分但不改持续扣分。

---

## 优先级建议（修改排序）

1. **A1: ARC-Easy per-example**（唯一实质提分项，~6-8h GPU）
2. **A2: Formal multiplicity metrics**（在 A1 数据出来后同时做 LAMBADA + ARC-Easy，~2h 计算）
3. **A6: 措辞精度修复**（低成本，防止持续扣分）
4. **A4: 补引 Fehlauer + Sellam**（均真实，增强 positioning）
5. **A3: BH 一句话**（已完成分析，论文加一句）
6. **A7: GDN 扩展**（如果 §4.5 已充分则 skip）
7. **A5: 标题**（取决于 A1 结果）
8. **A8+A9: rebuttal points**（最后准备）

---

## 遗漏问题

**上一轮一致性检查的 §4.3 hurt normalized ES 值（0.047）仍需修正**。如果 Agent 已修正，需确认。

````json
{
  "project": "entrainment_gated_ft",
  "text": "审稿回应 — ARC-Easy Per-Example 分析（最高优先级，D015）\n\nRound 2 审稿仍 3.0，核心瓶颈是证据仅 LAMBADA。需要扩展 per-example 分析到 ARC-Easy。\n\n## 任务：重跑 ARC-Easy 评估并提取 per-example 数据\n\n### Step 1: 确认数据状态\n先检查服务器上是否已有 per-example 输出：\n```bash\nfind /root/hybrid-model-zoo/ -name \"*.jsonl\" -path \"*arc_easy*\" 2>/dev/null | head -20\nfind /root/hybrid-model-zoo/ -name \"*log_samples*\" 2>/dev/null | head -20\n```\n\n如果没有 per-example 数据，需要重跑。\n\n### Step 2: 重跑 ARC-Easy 评估（带 --log_samples）\n对 1.3B 的全部 15 个模型（3 arch × 5 attention ratio）运行 lm-eval-harness：\n```bash\nlm_eval --model hf --model_args pretrained=&lt;model_path> --tasks arc_easy --batch_size 16 --log_samples --output_path /root/hybrid-model-zoo/eval_results_per_example/&lt;model_name>/\n```\n\n模型路径在 /root/hybrid-model-zoo/ 下。注意：\n- 所有 15 个 1.3B 模型：{GDN, RetNet, HGRN2} × {pure, h24-1, h12-1, h6-1, h3-1}\n- ARC-Easy 有 2376 道题\n- 每个模型预估 15-20 分钟，15 个模型串行约 4-5 小时\n- 可以用 nohup 后台运行一个 bash loop\n\n### Step 3: 提取 per-example correctness\n从 log_samples 输出中提取每个样本的 correct/incorrect：\n- 对每个 (architecture, attention_ratio) 组合，生成 correctness vector [0/1] for 2376 examples\n- 保存为 CSV: example_id, GDN_pure, GDN_h24, ..., HGRN2_h3\n\n### Step 4: 计算分析\n对 ARC-Easy 复制 LAMBADA 的完整分析链：\n1. 每个 architecture 的 helped/hurt/unchanged（pure → h3-1）\n2. Pairwise Jaccard（helped + hurt）across all 3 pairs × 5 ratios\n3. J_null, J_max, normalized ES\n4. Cross-architecture σ at each attention ratio\n5. Spearman ρ between attention ratio and σ\n6. **Formal multiplicity metrics（Marx 2020）**：\n   - Ambiguity(x) = 1 if 任意 2 个 architecture 不同意\n   - Overall ambiguity per attention ratio\n   - Discrepancy = max prob difference per example\n\n### Step 5: 同时在 LAMBADA 上计算 formal multiplicity metrics\n用已有的 LAMBADA per-example 数据计算 ambiguity 和 discrepancy。\n\n所有输出保存到 artifacts/paper_data/:\n- arc_easy_per_example.csv\n- arc_easy_jaccard_analysis.json\n- formal_multiplicity_metrics.json (LAMBADA + ARC-Easy)\n\n### 关键 Hypothesis\n**Per-example divergence (低 Jaccard) 在 ARC-Easy 上也存在**，即使 aggregate convergence 不显著。如果成立，论文 narrative 从 \"LAMBADA observation\" 升级为 \"per-example divergence is universal, aggregate convergence is task-specific\"。\n\n完成后汇报完整结果 + 数据文件路径。这是当前最高优先级任务。"
}
````

```json
{
  "text": "[Patch — Round 2 措辞修复 + 补引] EMNLP 2026 ARR June。Round 2 审稿仍 3.0。以下修改不依赖 Agent 的 ARC-Easy 数据，立即执行。\n\n引用需要验证。\n\n---\n\n## A3: BH 校正一句话\n§3.5 或 §4.1，在 Bonferroni 提及附近加一句：\n\"We also considered false discovery rate control (Benjamini--Hochberg); the adjusted $p$-value (0.102) does not differ from the Bonferroni correction, as only one of six benchmarks approaches nominal significance.\"\n\n## A4: 补引 2 篇\n§2 Related Work:\n\n1. **Fehlauer, Mahowald & Pimentel (EMNLP 2025)** \"Convergence and Divergence of Language Models under Different Random Seeds\" — arXiv:2509.26643, ACL Anthology 2025.emnlp-main.1675\n   - 加入 \"Underspecification and Model Agreement\" 段落\n   - 定位：他们研究 same-architecture different-seed 的 convergence，发现 larger models reconverge faster（四阶段 pattern）。我们研究 cross-architecture same-training 的 convergence。差异化一句话：\"Fehlauer et al. (2025) study convergence across random seeds within the same architecture, finding that larger models reconverge faster; we study convergence across different architectures with matched training, finding task-specific convergence.\"\n\n2. **Sellam et al. (ICLR 2022)** \"The MultiBERTs: BERT Reproductions for Robustness Analysis\" — arXiv:2106.16163\n   - 加入同段落\n   - 定位：25 个 BERT seeds + per-example robustness 分析。注意 Alexander D'Amour 是共同作者（与 underspecification 论文桥梁）\n   - 一句话：\"Sellam et al. (2022) released 25 BERT checkpoints to quantify per-example robustness under seed variation; our setting replaces seed variation with architectural variation.\"\n\n## A6: 措辞精度修复（4 处）\n\n1. **W5 \"nearly disjoint\"**: 全文搜索 \"nearly disjoint\" 或 \"almost entirely disjoint\"，改为 \"low overlap, approximately twice the independence baseline\"。检查 Abstract, Introduction, §4.3, §5。\n\n2. **W7 Attention overreach**: §4.4 或 experiments 中 \"Per-example effects originate primarily in the recurrent layers\" → \"Per-example effects are not explained by attention weight patterns, consistent with originating in the recurrent layers\"\n\n3. **W2 Scale 一致性**: 检查 Introduction contributions 列表和 §4.2 措辞是否统一使用 \"preliminary observation\"（不要一处说 \"we characterize\" 另一处说 \"preliminary\"）\n\n4. **Abstract**: 检查是否有 \"nearly disjoint\" 或 \"per-example specificity is fixed\"（过强），适度降级\n\n---\n\n完成后编译确认，汇报修改列表。"
}
```

**W5 "nearly disjoint" → "low overlap"**：
- Abstract: "nearly disjoint" → "show low overlap...approximately twice the independence baseline"

**W2 Scale 一致性**：

**Abstract 过强措辞**：

(2025) 的 controlled model zoo，在 10 个 benchmark 上评估 aggregate convergence，并在 LAMBADA 上进行 5,153 个样本的 per-example decomposition。核心发现：(1) 仅 LAMBADA 展示 cross-architecture convergence（ρ=−1.0，但 Bonferroni 后 p=.100）；(2) convergence 在 1.3B 单调，340M 非单调；(3) aggregate convergence 与 per-example divergence（Jaccard ≤ 0.11）解耦——前者随 attention ratio 递增，后者在 4% attention 时即饱和。 Strengths 1.  实验设计干净 : Controlled model zoo 排除训练数据和超参数差异的混淆，isolate 了 architecture 和 attention ratio 的效果（§3.1）。[ R1, R2 共识 ] 2.  统计报告透明度异常高 : 主动报告 Bonferroni 后不显著（p=.100）、bootstrap degenerate resamples（11.25%）、GDN head-count confound、normalized ES（5-7%）——这种透明度在 NLP 论文中罕见。[ R1, R2 共识 ] 3.  Per-example decomposition 分析视角有价值 : Helped/hurt/unchanged 分类 + Jaccard overlap + normalized effect size 是一套系统的分析工具。[ R1, R2 共识 ] 4.  Appendix robustness checks 充分 : GDN exclusion sensitivity、attention weight analysis、cross-scale Jaccard、permutation independence test、confidence margin analysis（Appendices B-K）。[ R1, R2 共识 ] 5.  Decoupling 发现有新意 : Aggregate convergence 和 per-example divergence 在不同时间尺度运作的发现，如果能在更多 benchmark 上验证，对 practitioners 有重要意义。[ R1 肯定; R2 部分肯定 ] 6.  可复现性高 : 基于公开 model zoo + lm-evaluation-harness 标准评估。[ R1, R2 共识 ] Weaknesses Must-Fix（影响接收决定） N1  [ critical | statistical_ methodology]  统计功效天花板：设计层面不可能达到校正后显著性 n=5 attention ratios 的精确排列检验最小可达 p=2/120=0.0167；Bonferroni 校正 6 个 confirmatory benchmarks 后 ≥ 0.100。即使 ρ=−1.0（最极端结果），在实验设计层面也不可能达到 α=0.05。论文转向 n=3 架构 bootstrap CI 作为\\\"complementary characterization\\\"（§4.1），但该 CI 仅有 24 种非退化组合（3^3=27 中 3 种退化，占 11.1%），BCa 在边界值退化回退到 percentile CI，n=3 无渐近覆盖保证。p 值不显著时依赖 CI，实质上是 post-hoc 证据标准切换。[ R1-N1, R1-N2; R2 [ AGREE] ] N2  [ critical | generalizability]  核心发现仅在 1/10 benchmarks 上成立，标题暗示更广适用性 10 个 benchmark 中仅 LAMBADA 表现出 monotonic convergence（ρ=−1.0），其余 9 个 |ρ| ≤ 0.7，p ≥ .233。Bonferroni 后 LAMBADA 也不显著（p=.100）。标题 \\\"Task-Specific Convergence\\\" 暗示按任务区分的系统性规律，实际是 1 个正例 + 9 个零结果。Decoupling claim（标题级发现）完全建立在 LAMBADA 这一个统计证据不充分的 benchmark 上——如果 convergence 不成立，decoupling 前提也不成立。[ R1-N3, R2-N1; 双方交叉 [ AGREE] ] N3  [ critical | related_ work]  遗漏关键相关工作 -  Fehlauer, Mahowald & Pimentel (EMNLP 2025)  \\\"Convergence and Divergence of LMs under Different Random Seeds\\\"——同 venue 前一年，直接研究 LM convergence/divergence，发现 larger models reconverge faster。R2 认为 must-cite + novelty 重叠；R1 认为 should-cite（不同 variation source：seed vs architecture），但 双方均同意应引用 。 -  Sellam et al. (ICLR 2022)  MultiBERTs——25 个不同 seed 的 BERT 模型 + per-example robustness 分析。[ R1, R2 共识 should-cite ] Reviewer Consensus（AGREE） 以下问题两位 Reviewer 在交叉回应中达成共识： W1  [ warning | confound]  GDN h3-1 的 16-head confound 应在正文讨论 GDN h3-1 在 1.3B 使用 16 attention heads（vs 其他 32）。移除 GDN 后 LAMBADA ρ 从 −1.0 降至 −0.7（p=.233），MMLU 方向反转。§4.5 RetNet-HGRN2 pair 部分缓解（σ 下降 54%），但 \\\"monotonic convergence at 1.3B\\\" 叙事不再完全成立。目前仅在 Appendix C 讨论。[ R1-W1; R2 [ AGREE] 并从 Minor 升级 ] W2  [ warning | overclaim]  Scale claim 过度推断 \\\"Scale as necessary condition\\\" 基于 2 个 data points（340M, 1.3B）。Introduction 用 \\\"We characterize\\\" 提升为 contribution，但 §4.2 自己承认 \\\"a preliminary observation from two data points\\\"。措辞不一致。[ R1-W4, R2-W1; 双方 [ AGREE] ] W3  [ warning | framing]  标题 scope 过大 \\\"Hybrid Recurrent Architectures\\\" 暗示普适覆盖，实际仅 3 个架构 + 1 个核心 benchmark。R1 建议 \\\"...Evidence from LAMBADA\\\"；R2 建议 \\\"A Case Study of Three...\\\"。[ R2-W3; R1 [ AGREE with nuance] ] W4  [ warning | methodology]  Predictive Multiplicity 框架应用不严格 Jaccard similarity 是 overlap proxy，不是 formal multiplicity metric。至少应计算 ambiguity（Marx et al. 2020: fraction of examples where models disagree）或 discrepancy。标题写入 \\\"Predictive Multiplicity\\\" 但未使用任何 formal metric。[ R2-W4; R1 [ AGREE] ] W5  [ warning | experimental_ validity]  Jaccard \\\"nearly disjoint\\\" 表述过度 J_ obs ≈ 0.10 仅为 J_ null ≈ 0.05 的 2 倍；normalized ES 仅 5-7% of theoretical maximum。\\\"Nearly disjoint\\\" 过度简化——更准确是 \\\"low overlap, approximately twice independence baseline\\\"。[ R1-W3; R2 [ AGREE] ] W6  [ warning | impact]  使用他人 model zoo 的贡献边界 无新训练、新数据、干预实验。纯分析贡献需更强统计证据或更广实证覆盖来补偿。[ R1-W2; R2 [ AGREE] ] W7  [ warning | overclaim]  Attention weight analysis 结论过强 Cosine similarity、entropy、last-5 attention mass 均为 null results。\\\"Per-example effects originate primarily in recurrent layers\\\"（§4.4）是排除法推断，无直接 recurrent layer 分析。应改为 \\\"are not explained by attention layers, consistent with originating in...\\\"。[ R1-W5; R2 [ AGREE] ] Reviewer Divergence（分歧） N4  [ R2: critical; R1: should-fix | novelty]  新颖性评估分歧 -  R2 观点 : 论文本质上是 D'Amour et al. (2022) underspecification 在 hybrid recurrent 设定下的验证，使用他人 model zoo + 标准方法，无方法论增量。Predictive multiplicity 写入标题但未使用 formal metric。构成 critical 问题。 -  R1 反驳 : (1) Variation source 是 architectural backbone 而非 random seed，不是 underspecification 的复现；(2) Dataset Cartography (Swayamdipta et al., 2020, EMNLP) 是纯观察性分析被接收的先例；(3) 但同意 formal multiplicity metric 缺失是 should-fix。 -  Director 判断 : 两种评估均有合理依据。R2 的 critical 评级基于更严格的增量标准；R1 的 should-fix 基于分析论文先例。记录分歧供 Research Director 判断。 Questions 1.  是否考虑 FDR 控制（Benjamini-Hochberg）替代 Bonferroni？BH 校正下 LAMBADA 的 adjusted p ≈ 0.017 可能通过 α=0.05。 2.  对 ARC-Easy（ρ=−0.7）和 MMLU（ρ=−0.7）能否做类似 LAMBADA 的 per-example 分析？即使 convergence 不显著，Jaccard saturation 的测试仍有价值——如果在非 convergence benchmark 上也观察到类似的 per-example overlap 模式，能提供有意义的对照。 3.  GDN confidence margin 分析为何使用 h12-1 而非 h3-1（Appendix K）？如果因 16-head confound，请在正文说明。 4.  是否有计划公开分析代码和 per-example 分类数据？ Missing References External Review (Stanford Agentic Reviewer) 外部审稿：未请求（项目未配置 external_ review）。 Visual Layout Check（非评分项） 视觉审查已完成。发现摘要： -  Critical（1） : 末页（p.15）约 75% 为空白，仅一张表格占底部——视觉上不专业，应重新排布 -  Warning（4） :   -  pp.3-5 连续 3 页纯文本（Related Work + Method），无任何 display item（figure/table/equation）——审稿人易失去注意力   -  主正文 8 页仅 3 个 display items（Figure 1, Figure 2, Table 1），远低于实证论文常见密度（5-8/页）；核心发现以文字呈现而 Appendix 堆积 14 张表   -  Method 部分核心定义（σ reduction, Jaccard, convergence ratio）仅以 inline math 呈现，缺少 numbered display equations   -  Conclusion + Limitations 页纯文本无 visual element -  Info（6） : Figure 1 位置良好但 axis label 偏小；Figure 2 受限单栏；Table 1 格式规范；Appendix 表格密度高但可读；References 格式统一无错误 Overall Assessment Overall Score: 3.0（Findings） 综合评价 : 论文提出了一个有价值的研究问题（architectural predictive multiplicity），分析方法系统且统计报告透明度异常高。Decoupling 发现——aggregate convergence 和 per-example divergence 的时间尺度分离——在概念上是论文最有趣的贡献。 但三个核心问题严重限制了当前版本的 impact： 1.  统计功效先天不足 （n=5 + Bonferroni = 设计层面不可能显著），且 CI 替代证据基于 n=3 bootstrap，形成两端不牢靠的证据链 2.  Single-benchmark scope : 标题级 claim 建立在单一 benchmark（LAMBADA）的非显著统计结果上 3.  GDN confound : 移除有混淆变量的架构后核心结论显著弱化（ρ: −1.0 → −0.7） 论文更适合  Findings track 。统计报告的诚实和分析的系统性值得肯定，但 scope 过窄和统计局限性限制了 conference-level impact。 Rebuttal 提升路径 （如全部解决，评分可望提升至 3.5 Borderline Conference）： -  (a) 扩展 per-example 分析到 ARC-Easy/MMLU 以测试 decoupling 泛化性 -  (b) 引入 formal multiplicity metric（ambiguity/discrepancy） -  (c) 采用 BH 替代 Bonferroni -  (d) 补引 Fehlauer et al. (2025) 的 hybrid recurrent model zoo（GatedDeltaNet/RetNet/HGRN2 × 5 种 attention ratio × 2 种 scale），在 10 个 benchmark 上系统分析 attention 对跨架构一致性的影响。核心发现：在 LAMBADA 上，aggregate 层面跨架构方差随 attention 单调递减（ρ = −1.0, BCa 95% CI [ −1.0, −0.7]），但 per-example helped/hurt set 的 Jaccard similarity 在最低 attention 比例即饱和（~ 0.10），不再随 attention 增加提升。作者将此称为 \\\"decoupling\\\"，连接到 underspecification 框架。 Strengths 1.  研究问题有价值且切入角度新颖 ：aggregate convergence vs per-example divergence 的分离框架连接了 underspecification 文献和 hybrid model 设计实践，cross-architecture dose-response 分析是既有文献（D'Amour et al., McCoy et al.）未涉及的维度。 2.  受控实验设计 ：使用共享 model zoo 消除训练数据和超参数混淆变量，隔离 attention ratio 效应，实验控制得当。 3.  统计方法论标准高 ：exact permutation test（120 种排列全枚举）、BCa bootstrap CI（10,000 重采样）、Bonferroni 校正、permutation test for Jaccard，在分析性论文中属于高标准。 4.  高透明度 ：Limitations 部分主动讨论 6 项局限（统计功效、scope、scale、架构覆盖、观察性设计、仅 pre-training），Appendix A-G 提供丰富的 robustness check 和 sensitivity analysis。 5.  写作清晰 ：Figure 1 三面板设计有效传达核心发现，叙事结构逻辑通顺。 Weaknesses Must-Fix（影响接收决定） MF-1 [统计功效根本性不足]  ⟨R1-N1, R1-N2 | 共识⟩\\n 核心定量 claim（ρ = −1.0）基于 n=5 attention ratios，Bonferroni 校正后 adjusted p = 0.100 未达 α = 0.05。作者转向 CI 作为 primary evidence，但这是 post-hoc 的证据标准切换。更严重的是，替代证据——n=3 架构级 bootstrap CI——仅有 24 种非退化组合（3³ - 3 = 24），10,000 次迭代只是在这 24 种结果中反复抽样，CI 宽度不携带有意义的不确定性信息。核心统计证据链在两个关键环节上同时不可靠。 MF-2 [Decoupling claim 可能是 floor effect]  ⟨R1-N3 | 共识⟩\\n Jaccard ~ 0.094–0.104 仅为随机基线（~ 0.039）的约 2.5 倍。Helped set 仅占总样本 9–12%（~ 400–600/5,153），两个同等大小弱相关集合的 Jaccard 自然停留在低水平。\\\"饱和\\\" 可能只是 \\\"从未离开过底部\\\"。论文未计算 Jaccard 理论上界，也未报告相对于基线的 effect size，decoupling claim 缺乏充分支撑。 MF-3 [GDN confound 实质性削弱核心结果]  ⟨R1-N4 | 共识⟩\\n GDN h3-1 使用 16 heads（vs 其他配置 32 heads）。移除 GDN 后 LAMBADA ρ 从 −1.0 降至 −0.7（p = .233），MMLU 从 −0.7 反转为 +0.6。核心 convergence 发现在很大程度上受单一有混淆变量的架构驱动。此分析仅在 Appendix C，正文讨论严重不足。 MF-4 [泛化性严重不足]  ⟨R2-N1, R1-W1 | 共识⟩\\n 10 个 benchmark 中仅 LAMBADA 一个展示 monotonic convergence，且该 benchmark 的 Bonferroni 校正后 p = .100 亦未通过严格阈值。即便仅看 6 个 pre-registered benchmark 也仅 1/6 命中，兼容 family-wise error rate 控制后的偶然发现解释。标题和 abstract 暗示通用发现，与证据覆盖范围不匹配。 MF-5 [缺少关键相关工作]  ⟨R2-N3 | 共识⟩\\n 遗漏三条重要文献线：(1) Waleffe et al. (2024) \\\"An Empirical Study of Mamba-based Language Models\\\"——8B 规模系统比较 hybrid 架构的里程碑工作；(2) Predictive multiplicity 文献（Marx et al. 2020; Watson-Daniels et al. 2023; Hsu & Calmon 2022）——cross-architecture prediction 分歧正是 predictive multiplicity 的定义，概念匹配度高于 underspecification；(3) Basu (2025) \\\"When Does Content-Based Routing Work?\\\"——直接研究 hybrid 模型中 attention 分配。 Should-Fix SF-1  ⟨W-scale | 共识⟩ Scale comparison 仅 340M 和 1.3B 两个点，\\\"sufficient scale as necessary condition\\\" 结论过度，应降级为 preliminary observation。 SF-2  ⟨W-title | 共识⟩ 标题 scope 远大于实际贡献。建议加入 \\\"on LAMBADA\\\"、\\\"A Case Study\\\" 或其他限定语。 SF-3  ⟨W-preregistration | 共识⟩ Pre-registration 声称仅在 Appendix A，无外部可验证性。应提供 OSF 时间戳或改为 confirmatory/exploratory split 措辞。 SF-4  ⟨W-entropy | 共识⟩ HGRN2 ΔH = +0.0420 vs GDN −0.0009（~ 47 倍差异），\\\"all negligible\\\" 的 blanket conclusion 忽略架构间异质性，应分架构讨论。 SF-5  ⟨W-practical | 部分共识⟩ Practical implications 薄弱，缺乏具体可操作的架构选择或 ensemble 策略建议（R1 部分反驳：ARR 不应因缺少 recipe 惩罚分析论文）。 Reviewer Consensus [ AGREE ] 两位 Reviewer 在以下问题上完全达成一致： 1.  统计证据链结构性薄弱 ：n=5 检验功效不足 + n=3 bootstrap 退化 + Bonferroni 后不显著，三重叠加使核心 claim 的统计基础不稳固 2.  Jaccard floor effect 是 decoupling claim 的关键替代解释 ，论文未排除 3.  GDN confound 必须在正文充分讨论 ，当前仅在 Appendix 不可接受 4.  泛化性不足 ：1/10（或 1/6 pre-registered）benchmark 支撑核心 claim 5.  Scale 结论过度 ：2 个数据点不支撑 \\\"necessary condition\\\" 声明 6.  缺少 predictive multiplicity 文献线和 Waleffe et al. 7.  标题 scope 需缩窄 Reviewer Divergence [ REBUT ] 1.  新颖性评估 ：R1 认为 cross-architecture dose-response 分析维度有框架增量（≠ D'Amour/McCoy 的同架构不同 seed），excitement 可达 3/5；R2 认为本质是已知现象在新领域的验证，excitement 仅 2/5。双方均同意缺乏机制解释是共同弱点。 2.  引用数量 ：R2 认为 30 篇偏少；R1 认为是质量问题而非数量问题，补上遗漏的 3-5 篇即可。 3.  纯分析论文定位 ：R2 认为无训练/无新方法需额外强 insight 补偿；R1 认为 ARR guidelines (H5, H7) 不应因论文类型惩罚。 Questions（需作者在 rebuttal 中回答） 1.  能否计算 Jaccard 理论上界并报告相对 effect size，以区分 \\\"true saturation\\\" 和 \\\"floor effect\\\"？ 2.  能否补充 example-level bootstrap CI（利用现有 5,153 个样本）替代退化的 architecture-level bootstrap？ 3.  移除 GDN 后，RetNet-HGRN2 两架构的 per-example decoupling 是否仍然成立？ 4.  是否存在其他 retrieval 类 benchmark（如 Natural Questions, TriviaQA）可用于验证 LAMBADA 发现的复现性？ 5.  相对于 McCoy et al. (2020) 的具体增量贡献是什么——是架构类型不同、attention ratio 连续变量、还是 timescale 分离？ 6.  Underspecification 框架 vs predictive multiplicity 框架，哪个更精确匹配本文的 cross-architecture 设定？ Missing References 1.  Waleffe et al. (2024)  \\\"An Empirical Study of Mamba-based Language Models\\\" — 8B 规模 hybrid 架构系统比较 2.  Marx et al. (2020)  \\\"Predictive Multiplicity in Classification\\\" — predictive multiplicity 奠基文献 3.  Watson-Daniels et al. (2023)  \\\"Predictive Multiplicity in Probabilistic Classification\\\" — predictive multiplicity 扩展 4.  Hsu & Calmon (2022)  \\\"Rashomon Capacity: A Metric for Predictive Multiplicity in Classification\\\" — multiplicity 度量 5.  Basu (2025)  \\\"When Does Content-Based Routing Work?\\\" — hybrid 模型 attention 分配 External Review (Stanford Agentic Reviewer) 状态：未返回（pending） 外部审稿请求已于 2026-05-20T04:28 发送，截至报告投递时仍处于 pending 状态。外部结果到达后将作为补充材料发送至 Group C。 Visual Layout Check（非评分项） 状态：已完成  | 0 critical / 3 warning / 7 info Warnings 1. Page 14 ：末页仅约 1/3 有内容，下方约 2/3 空白，附录空间未充分利用。 Info -  Figure 1 子图 axis label 和 legend 字号偏小（约正文 60%） -  Figure 2 数据点密集，部分重叠，考虑全宽展示或加 jitter -  第 1 页无视觉锚点，Figure 1 在第 2 页顶部，建议提升至第 1 页底部 -  Table 1 格式规范，LAMBADA 行已加粗标注 -  Table 2 Net 列 +/- 数值未加粗标注极值 -  References 格式一致，无 [ ? ] 未解析引用 -  附录表格格式规范 Overall Assessment Overall Score: 3.0 / 5 — Findings 评分理由 ： 研究问题有价值（aggregate vs per-example 行为的分离），实验设计利用受控 model zoo 消除混淆变量，统计方法论框架在意图上严谨。但核心统计证据链存在结构性弱点：(1) n=5 检验 Bonferroni 后不显著且 p→CI 转向是 post-hoc；(2) n=3 bootstrap CI 退化；(3) decoupling claim 的 Jaccard 证据可能是 floor effect；(4) GDN confound 使核心结果受单一架构驱动；(5) 仅 1/10 benchmark 支撑核心 claim。这些问题叠加后，证据力度不足以支撑主会议（4.0）接收，但研究问题的价值和分析框架的创意使其适合 Findings track。 置信度 : 4/5（两位 Reviewer 均熟悉统计方法和 hybrid architecture 文献） Rebuttal 可能上调条件 （R2 明确提出）：提供 Jaccard 理论上界分析 + 外部交叉验证 + 正文讨论 GDN confound → 可考虑上调至 3.5（Borderline Conference）。 Detailed Comments Section 1 (Introduction) -  Figure 1 有效传达核心发现，但首页无视觉元素，建议提升 -  Contribution bullets 准确但 scope 暗示过于通用 Section 2 (Related Work) -  缺少 predictive multiplicity 文献线（与 underspecification 有关键区别：后者是同一 pipeline 不同 run，前者关注不同模型间的 prediction 分歧，更匹配本文设定） -  缺少 Waleffe et al. (2024) 和 Basu (2025) -  现有引用覆盖面在 hybrid architecture 和 per-example analysis 方面合理 Section 3 (Method) -  Task taxonomy pre-registration 声称需外部可验证性 -  Pages 3-5 纯文本缺乏视觉锚点，建议加入架构概览图 Section 4 (Experiments) -  Table 1：应同时报告含/不含 GDN 的结果 -  Section 4.3-4.4：Jaccard floor effect 需正面讨论 -  Section 4.4：HGRN2 和 GDN 的 entropy 差异不应用 blanket \\\"negligible\\\" 概括 -  Section 4.5：Robustness analysis 揭示 GDN 依赖性，应在正文（非仅 Appendix）充分讨论 Section 5 (Conclusion) -  \\\"sufficient scale as necessary condition\\\" 措辞应降级 -  Future work 方向合理但 practical implications 可更具体 Limitations -  坦诚全面，是论文的一个亮点 Appendix -
